1: # Implementation Plan - Classroom Window Border Padding & 3-Group Assignment Layout
2: 
3: Fix the unwanted border padding on smaller desktop displays for the Google Classroom window, and restructure the To-Do assignments section to categorize tasks into three distinct groups (Assigned / No Due Date, Upcoming / Yet to pass due date, and Passed due date with a small-height divider banner), plus a clean section for Completed tasks.
4: 
5: ## User Review Required
6: 
7: > [!NOTE]
8: > - **Group 1**: Assigned tasks (no due date set) displayed at the top.
9: > - **Group 2**: Tasks that have yet to pass their due date (upcoming deadlines), sorted with earliest due dates first.
10: > - **Group 3**: Tasks that have passed their due date (missing/overdue), divided by a compact, high-contrast banner header.
11: > - **Completed Group**: Tasks that are turned in, graded, or returned will display neatly below with their own completed divider.
12: 
13: ## Proposed Changes
14: 
15: ### CSS Stylesheets
16: 
17: #### [MODIFY] [css/responsive.css](file:///c:/Users/gsmur/OneDrive/Documents/GitHub/b1t-Sched/css/responsive.css)
18: - Exclude `.classroom-modal-content` / `#classroom-modal .modal-content` from generic `.modal-content` padding (`var(--spacing-lg)` and `var(--spacing-md)`) in both `@media (min-width: 769px) and (max-width: 1366px)` and `@media (min-width: 769px) and (max-height: 768px)`.
19: - Ensure `#classroom-modal .modal-content` retains `padding: 0 !important;`, `width: 90%`, `max-width: 1000px`, `height: 80vh`, and `overflow: hidden;`.
20: 
21: #### [MODIFY] [css/classroom.css](file:///c:/Users/gsmur/OneDrive/Documents/GitHub/b1t-Sched/css/classroom.css)
22: - Ensure `#classroom-modal .modal-content` and `.classroom-modal-content` explicitly set `padding: 0 !important;`.
23: - Add compact divider banner styles:
24:   - `.classroom-group-divider`, `.passed-due-divider`, `.completed-divider`
25:   - `.classroom-group-divider-badge`, `.classroom-group-divider-line`, `.classroom-group-count`
26:   - Complete theme support for Default (Maroon/White), Dark Mode, and Gray Mode.
27: 
28: ---
29: 
30: ### Google Classroom Logic
31: 
32: #### [MODIFY] [js/classroom.js](file:///c:/Users/gsmur/OneDrive/Documents/GitHub/b1t-Sched/js/classroom.js)
33: - Add date & status helper functions:
34:   - `getAssignmentDueDate(item)`
35:   - `isPastDue(item, now)`
36:   - `isSubmittedOrReturned(item)`
37:   - `getAssignmentGroup(item, now)`
38: - Update `sortAssignments(assignments)` to sort items sequentially:
39:   1. Group 0: Assigned (No due date)
40:   2. Group 1: Due date yet to pass (ascending by due date)
41:   3. Group 2: Passed due date (ascending by due date)
42:   4. Group 3: Completed (Turned In, Graded, Returned)
43: - Add `renderTodoList(items, renderItemFn)` to render the grouped layout with the small-height "Passed Due Date" divider banner and "Completed" divider banner.
44: - Update `renderAllItems` and `renderCourseDetails` when `viewType === 'todo'` to use `renderTodoList`.
45: 
46: ## Verification Plan
47: 
48: ### Automated / Node Verification
49: - Run a standalone Node.js test script to verify `sortAssignments`, `getAssignmentDueDate`, `isPastDue`, and `renderTodoList` grouping logic across mock assignments with various due dates, missing due dates, past due dates, and completion statuses.
50: 
51: ### Manual Verification
52: - Test responsive view at screen widths between 769px and 1366px and heights <= 768px to ensure the Classroom window renders flush without any border padding around the header or body.
53: - Verify the To-Do view in both "All Courses" and individual course view:
54:   1. Top: Assigned tasks without due dates.
55:   2. Middle: Active tasks due in the future.
56:   3. Bottom: "Passed Due Date" small banner followed by overdue tasks.
57:   4. Followed by Completed tasks with their completed divider banner.

1: # Walkthrough - Classroom Window Padding & 3-Group Assignment Layout
2: 
3: ## Overview
4: We fixed the unwanted border padding on smaller desktop displays for the Google Classroom modal and reorganized the Assignments (To-Do) view to divide tasks into 3 distinct vertical groups, with a small-height divider banner separating tasks that have passed their due dates.
5: 
6: ---
7: 
8: ## Changes
9: 
10: ### CSS Stylesheets
11: 
12: #### [css/responsive.css](file:///c:/Users/gsmur/OneDrive/Documents/GitHub/b1t-Sched/css/responsive.css)
13: - Excluded `#classroom-modal .modal-content` and `.classroom-modal-content` from generic `.modal-content` padding (`var(--spacing-lg)` and `var(--spacing-md)`).
14: - Enforced `padding: 0 !important;`, `width: 90%`, `max-width: 1000px`, `height: 80vh`, and `overflow: hidden;` across standard smaller desktop displays (769px–1366px) and height-constrained screens (max-height: 768px).
15: 
16: #### [css/classroom.css](file:///c:/Users/gsmur/OneDrive/Documents/GitHub/b1t-Sched/css/classroom.css)
17: - Added `padding: 0 !important;` to `.classroom-modal-content` and `#classroom-modal .modal-content`.
18: - Styled compact group divider banners (`.classroom-group-divider`, `.passed-due-divider`, `.completed-divider`, `.classroom-group-divider-badge`, `.classroom-group-count`) with theme support for Default, Dark Mode, and Gray Mode.
19: 
20: ---
21: 
22: ### Google Classroom Logic
23: 
24: #### [js/classroom.js](file:///c:/Users/gsmur/OneDrive/Documents/GitHub/b1t-Sched/js/classroom.js)
25: - Implemented robust helper functions:
26:   - `getAssignmentDueDate(item)`: Robustly parses Google Classroom `dueDate` object, `Date` instances, and timestamp strings.
27:   - `isPastDue(item, now)`: Accurately checks whether the deadline has passed.
28:   - `isSubmittedOrReturned(item)`: Detects if the task is turned in, graded, or returned.
29:   - `getAssignmentGroup(item, now)`: Categorizes tasks into Group 0 (Assigned, no due date), Group 1 (Upcoming, yet to pass due date), Group 2 (Passed due date), and Group 3 (Completed).
30: - Updated `sortAssignments(assignments)` to sort assignments sequentially:
31:   1. **Group 1**: Assigned (No due date)
32:   2. **Group 2**: Yet to pass due date (ascending by deadline, earliest first)
33:   3. **Group 3**: Passed due date (ascending by deadline)
34:   4. **Group 4**: Completed (Turned In, Graded, Returned)
35: - Added `renderTodoList(items, renderItemFn)` to render the 3-group layout:
36:   - Assigned tasks (no due date) at the top.
37:   - Tasks that have yet to pass their due date directly below assigned tasks and above overdue tasks.
38:   - A small-height, clear **Passed Due Date** banner with task count introducing the overdue tasks.
39:   - A clean **Completed** banner if completed tasks exist.
40: - Updated `renderAllItems` and `renderCourseDetails` when `viewType === 'todo'` to use `renderTodoList`.
41: 
42: ---
43: 
44: ## Verification Results
45:

53: ✓ Test 4: Rendered HTML order and divider banners passed
54: ALL TESTS PASSED SUCCESSFULLY!
55: ```

169:     // Helper to retrieve data from in-memory cache, CacheManager, or JSON template cache
170:     async getOrFetchData(type) {
171:         let result = [];
172:         if (this.inMemoryCache && this.inMemoryCache[type] && this.inMemoryCache[type].length > 0) {
173:             result = this.inMemoryCache[type];
174:         } else {
175:             this.initCacheManager();
176:             if (this.cacheManager) {
177:                 const cached = await this.cacheManager.getCachedClassroomData(type);
178:                 if (cached && cached.data && cached.data.length > 0) {
179:                     if (!this.inMemoryCache) this.inMemoryCache = {};
180:                     this.inMemoryCache[type] = cached.data;
181:                     result = cached.data;
182:                 }
183:             }
184: 
185:             if (result.length === 0) {
186:                 const jsonCache = this.getJsonCache();
187:                 if (jsonCache && jsonCache[type]) {
188:                     if (!this.inMemoryCache) this.inMemoryCache = {};
189:                     this.inMemoryCache[type] = jsonCache[type];
190:                     result = jsonCache[type];
191:                 }
192:             }
193:         }
194: 
195:         if (type === 'assignments' && Array.isArray(result)) {
196:             result = this.sortAssignments(result);
197:             if (this.inMemoryCache) this.inMemoryCache.assignments = result;
198:         }
199: 
200:         return result || [];
201:     },
202: 
203:     // Extract and parse Date from assignment dueDate / dueTime
204:     getAssignmentDueDate(item) {
205:         if (!item || !item.dueDate) return null;
206:         if (typeof item.dueDate === 'object' && 'year' in item.dueDate) {
207:             return new Date(
208:                 item.dueDate.year,
209:                 item.dueDate.month - 1,
210:                 item.dueDate.day,
211:                 item.dueTime?.hours !== undefined ? item.dueTime.hours : 23,
212:                 item.dueTime?.minutes !== undefined ? item.dueTime.minutes : 59
213:             );
214:         }
215:         if (item.dueDate instanceof Date) return item.dueDate;
216:         if (typeof item.dueDate === 'string' || typeof item.dueDate === 'number') {
217:             const d = new Date(item.dueDate);
218:             if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) return d;
219:         }
220:         return null;
221:     },
222: 
223:     // Check if assignment has passed its due date
224:     isPastDue(item, now = new Date()) {
225:         const due = this.getAssignmentDueDate(item);
226:         if (!due) return false;
227:         return due < now;
228:     },
229: 
230:     // Check if assignment is submitted, turned in, graded, or returned
231:     isSubmittedOrReturned(item) {
232:         if (!item) return false;
233:         if (item.statusCode) {
234:             return item.statusCode === 'turned_in' || item.statusCode === 'returned' || item.statusCode === 'graded';
235:         }
236:         if (item.submissionState) {
237:             return item.submissionState === 'TURNED_IN' || item.submissionState === 'RETURNED';
238:         }
239:         if (item.status) {
240:             return item.status === 'Turned in' || item.status === 'Returned' || (typeof item.status === 'string' && item.status.startsWith('Graded')) || item.status === 'Turned in (Late)';
241:         }
242:         return false;
243:     },
244: 
245:     // Determine assignment group: 0 = Assigned (no due date), 1 = Due date yet to pass, 2 = Passed due date, 3 = Completed
246:     getAssignmentGroup(item, now = new Date()) {
247:         if (!item) return 0;
248:         if (this.isSubmittedOrReturned(item)) return 3;
249:         if (!item.dueDate) return 0;
250:         if (this.isPastDue(item, now)) return 2;
251:         return 1;
252:     },
253: 
254:     // Sort assignments into 3 vertical groups (+ completed):
255:     // 1. "Assigned" tasks (no due date)
256:     // 2. Tasks that have yet to pass due date (upcoming deadlines, ascending)
257:     // 3. Tasks that have passed due date (missing/overdue, ascending)
258:     // 4. Completed tasks (turned in, returned, graded, ascending)
259:     sortAssignments(assignments) {
260:         if (!Array.isArray(assignments)) return [];
261:         const now = new Date();
262: 
263:         return [...assignments].sort((a, b) => {
264:             const groupA = this.getAssignmentGroup(a, now);
265:             const groupB = this.getAssignmentGroup(b, now);
266: 
267:             if (groupA !== groupB) {
268:                 return groupA - groupB;
269:             }
270: 
271:             // Within the same group, maintain ascending due date order (earliest deadline first)
272:             const dateA = this.getAssignmentDueDate(a);
273:             const dateB = this.getAssignmentDueDate(b);
274: 
275:             if (!dateA && !dateB) return 0;
276:             if (!dateA) return 1;
277:             if (!dateB) return -1;
278:             return dateA - dateB;
279:         });
280:     },
281:

283:     updateBottomCachedFooter(show, timeLabel = '') {

1581:     // Render To-Do items divided into 3 distinct groups (+ completed) with small-height divider banners
1582:     renderTodoList(items, renderItemFn) {
1583:         if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) return '';
1584:         const now = new Date();
1585: 
1586:         const assignedNoDue = [];
1587:         const upcomingDue = [];
1588:         const passedDue = [];
1589:         const completed = [];
1590: 
1591:         items.forEach(item => {
1592:             const group = this.getAssignmentGroup(item, now);
1593:             if (group === 0) {
1594:                 assignedNoDue.push(item);
1595:             } else if (group === 1) {
1596:                 upcomingDue.push(item);
1597:             } else if (group === 2) {
1598:                 passedDue.push(item);
1599:             } else {
1600:                 completed.push(item);
1601:             }
1602:         });
1603: 
1604:         let html = '';
1605: 
1606:         // Group 1: Assigned tasks (no due date set)
1607:         if (assignedNoDue.length > 0) {
1608:             html += assignedNoDue.map(renderItemFn).join('');
1609:         }
1610: 
1611:         // Group 2: Tasks that have yet to pass the due date (upcoming deadlines)
1612:         if (upcomingDue.length > 0) {
1613:             html += upcomingDue.map(renderItemFn).join('');
1614:         }
1615: 
1616:         // Group 3: Tasks that have passed the due date (missing/overdue) - divided with small-height banner
1617:         if (passedDue.length > 0) {
1618:             html += `
1619:                 <div class="classroom-group-divider passed-due-divider">
1620:                     <div class="classroom-group-divider-line"></div>
1621:                     <div class="classroom-group-divider-badge">
1622:                         <i class="fa-solid fa-clock-rotate-left"></i>
1623:                         <span>Passed Due Date</span>
1624:                         <span class="classroom-group-count">${passedDue.length}</span>
1625:                     </div>
1626:                     <div class="classroom-group-divider-line"></div>
1627:                 </div>
1628:             `;
1629:             html += passedDue.map(renderItemFn).join('');
1630:         }
1631:

1633:         if (completed.length > 0) {
1634:             html += `

### Summary of Changes

1. **Prioritized Upcoming Deadlines at Top**:
   - In [`js/classroom.js`](file:///c:/Users/gsmur/Documents/GitHub/[oU1TS]/b1t-Sched/js/classroom.js), updated `getAssignmentGroup` and `sortAssignments` so tasks with active due dates that have **yet to pass the deadline** (Group 0) are prioritized at the top, sorted chronologically with the earliest upcoming deadline first.

2. **Divider Banner for Past Deadline Tasks**:
   - Updated the small-height banner text for overdue tasks from `"Passed Due Date"` to **`"Past deadline"`** with the count badge and clock icon.
   - Tasks that are past deadline appear below this banner, followed by completed tasks under the `"Completed"` banner.

3. **Documentation Update**:

1: # b1t-Sched - Academic Task Scheduler
2: 
3: A single-page application (SPA) that helps students manage academic tasks, assignments, exams, and events with personalized department-specific content.
4: 
5: <div align=center>
6: <img src="Social-Preview.webp" width=200px align=center>
7:    <h1>👉 <a href="https://github.com/b1tranger/b1t-Sched/blob/main/doc/DOCUMENTATION.md">Documentation</a> 👈</h1>
8:    </div>
9: <!-- ![b1t-Sched Preview]() -->
10: 
11: ## ✨ Features
12: 
13: - 📱 **Single-Page Application** - Fast, seamless navigation
14: - 🔐 **Firebase Authentication** - Secure email/password login with password reset
15: - 👤 **User Profiles** - Set department, semester, and section once
16: - 📋 **Personalized Tasks** - View tasks filtered by your academic details, with clickable links and collapsible descriptions
17: - ✏️ **Edit Entries** - Users can edit their own tasks; admins can edit all tasks and events; CRs can create department events and manage their own
18: - 🔗 **Dynamic Resource Links** - Department-specific routine, calendar, and faculty contacts with built-in PDF viewer
19: - 📅 **Event Calendar** - Track upcoming academic events with collapsible descriptions, department scope badge, and clickable links
20: - 🛡️ **UITS Event Raiders Live Feed** - Live syndication of hackathons, contests, olympiads, and symposiums from `ou1ts/events` RSS/JSON feeds with desktop sticky sidebar integration, mobile arrowhead view switcher with localStorage state persistence, and compact two-segment card layouts
21: - 📆 **Calendar View** - Interactive monthly calendar with task visualization, date navigation, and responsive mobile layout (monthly/weekly toggle)
22: - 📣 **CR Notices** - Class Representatives can post, edit, and delete notices visible to their section group; supports priority levels and deadline tracking
23: - 📰 **University Notice Viewer** - View UCAM university notices with PDF preview, powered by Vercel serverless backend with local caching
24: - 🏫 **Google Classroom Integration** - View assignments, announcements, and course materials in a unified interface with OAuth session persistence, status indicators (**Assigned**, **Missing**, **Turned in**, **Returned / Graded**), two-way task sync, and auto-completion tracking for turned-in work
25: - 📝 **Note Taking** - Personal notes with markdown support, auto-save, and file upload via catbox.moe/tmpfiles.org
26: - 📊 **Activity Timeline** - Visual heatmap and bar chart tracking user activity (logins, tasks, events) for productivity insights
27: - 🔔 **Push Notifications** - Real-time browser notifications for new tasks and events (mobile-compatible)
28: - 🏆 **Contributions** - Leaderboard of top contributors (group-specific or global across all departments)
29: - 📤 **Task Export** - Export tasks to TXT, Markdown (.md), or vector PDF with selectable black fonts, embedded clickable links, 120% scale, straight line dividers, active type filter support (`Exam`, `Assignment`, etc.), and dedicated Old Tasks pagination.
30: - 🧭 **Quick Navbar Logo Navigation** - Clickable brand logo and title to instantly navigate to Dashboard view from anywhere on the site with smooth scroll-to-top.
31: - 🎓 **Semester Auto-Promotion** - Automatic semester advancement every July and January on user login with 30-day manual profile protection and an Admin fail-safe bulk update tool (with 30s verification countdown & 6-month cooldown)
32: - ⚙️ **Profile Settings** - Update your details anytime (30-day cooldown)
33: - ❓ **FAQ Section** - Collapsible accordion explaining how the site works, user roles, and profile settings
34: - 🎨 **Maroon Theme** - Professional dark maroon and off-white color scheme
35: - 📱 **Fully Responsive** - Works on desktop, tablet, and mobile
36: - 📲 **Progressive Web App & Offline Experience** - Installable app with offline caching, background sync queue, and an adaptive offline banner that dynamically pushes all top-level content, navigation bars, and mobile sidebars down without overlaying UI elements
37: 
38: > 📖 For complete documentation, see [`doc/DOCUMENTATION.md`](doc/DOCUMENTATION.md)
39:

75:

77: - Modern web browser (Chrome, Firefox, Edge)
78: - Firebase account (free)

84:    - Complete all steps to set up Firebase project
85:    - Create Firestore collections with sample data
86:    - Copy your Firebase configuration

90: 1. Open `js/firebase-config.js`
91: 2. Replace the placeholder values with your Firebase configuration:
92: 
93: ```javascript

835: ---
836: 
837: ### 12. Classroom (classroom.js)
838: 
839: **Purpose:** Google Classroom API integration for fetching courses, assignments, announcements, and posted course materials.
840: 
841: **Authentication & Session Persistence:**
842: 
843: - Uses **Google Identity Services (GIS)** for OAuth 2.0 with scopes for courses, coursework, announcements, and coursework materials (`https://www.googleapis.com/auth/classroom.courseworkmaterials.readonly`).
844: - Implements a promise-based initialization (`init()`) synchronized with the main application loading screen.
845: - **Persistent Connection & Expired Token Banner**: The `classroom_connected` flag remains intact across access token expirations ($1\text{ hour}$). When a stored access token expires, `b1t-Sched` avoids disruptive background iframe/popup flashes and prevents blank unauthorized API views by immediately displaying cached assignments, announcements, and materials alongside a 1-click **Reconnect** banner. Clicking **Reconnect** calls `Classroom.login()`, opening the Google authentication prompt to obtain a fresh token without requiring manual sign-out.
846: - **Mobile Header Responsiveness**: On mobile screen sizes (`@media (max-width: 768px)` and mobile sidebar drawer), `.classroom-view-header` flexes vertically into a stacked 2-line layout. Line 1 displays the back button and full course title; line 2 displays full-width view toggle buttons (**To-Do**, **Notices**, **Materials**), eliminating horizontal title truncation and button crowding.
847: - **Unified Sign Out Button**: A single pill-styled Sign Out button (`.classroom-header-logout-btn`, `border-radius: 20px`) is embedded in top window title headers (`#classroom-sidebar` mobile and `#classroom-modal` desktop). The button is dynamically displayed only when logged in / connected. Explicit logout revokes the OAuth token, clears local storage connection flags, clears cache, and resets the view to the login prompt.
848: - **Archived Classrooms Management**: Fetches both active and archived courses (`courseStates=ACTIVE&courseStates=ARCHIVED`). Archived classrooms are hidden by default in the "My Classes" view, with a toggle button to expand/hide them. Unified feeds (**To-Do**, **Notices**, **Materials**) strictly filter out content from archived courses.
849: - **Client JSON Template Caching & Persistent Reconnect Footer**: Serializes user courses, assignments, notices, and materials into a structured JSON template (`classroom_cached_json`) saved to `localStorage` (mirrored in `sessionStorage`) for persistent offline access across browser tab closures and token expirations. When cached content is displayed due to an expired session, a sticky bottom footer bar (`#classroom-footer-mobile` and `#classroom-footer-desktop`) is rendered with a narrow banner (`"Cached Content, login again to see new data"`) and a persistent `"Reconnect Classroom"` button (`Classroom.login()`).
850: - **Real-Time Assignment Status Indicators**: Concurrently queries Google Classroom Student Submissions (`courses/{courseId}/courseWork/-/studentSubmissions?userId=me`) to render color-coded bottom-right badges on all To-Do items: **Assigned** (Blue), **Missing** (Red), **Turned in** / **Turned in (Late)** (Green), and **Returned** / **Graded: X/Y** (Purple).
851: - **Two-Group Vertical To-Do Arrangement**: Automatically groups assignments into two vertical sections: active/pending assignments at the top (so they are always immediately visible), followed by completed/turned-in assignments below, each sorted in ascending order of due dates.
852: - **Automatic Task Completion Checking**: Upon sign-in or loading classroom contents, assignments in `Turned in`, `Returned`, or `Graded` state automatically match and check/complete corresponding tasks in the user's personal "Pending Tasks" list.
853: - **Two-Way Task Sync (Add & Update)**: Clicking **Sync** for Admins/CRs creates new tasks from assignments and automatically updates existing tasks if a teacher modifies deadlines, titles, or instructions in Classroom.
854: - **Materials Change Detection**: Live updates to course materials (new Drive files, YouTube links, Forms, or descriptions) are dynamically recognized via `updateTime` comparison and rendered as interactive attachment pills.
855: 
856: **Properties:**
857: 
858: - `accessToken`: Current OAuth 2.0 access token.
859: - `courses`: Cached list of enrolled active and archived courses.
860: - `currentView`: Active view mode (`'todo'`, `'notifications'`, or `'materials'`).
861: - `showArchivedCourses`: Flag for toggling archived course card visibility in "My Classes".
862: - `hasExpiredSession`: Flag indicating cached display mode with re-connect banner.
863: - `JSON_CACHE_KEY`: Storage key for the JSON snapshot (`'classroom_cached_json'`).
864: - `_authResolve`: Internal resolver to signal authentication completion to the main app.
865: 
866: | Method                           | Parameters | Returns | Description                                                                                        |
867: | -------------------------------- | ---------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
868: | `init()`                         | -          | Promise | Initialize GIS client and check for persisted session.                                             |
869: | `checkPersistedSession()`        | -          | Promise | Check storage for valid token or attempt silent refresh without clearing connection state.          |
870: | `login()`                        | -          | void    | Trigger manual OAuth login popup.                                                                  |
871: | `handleAuthSuccess(response)`    | object     | void    | Handle successful token acquisition and start data fetching.                                       |
872: | `fetchCoursesAndLoadAll()`       | -          | void    | Batch load active/archived courses and load unified items based on `currentView`.                  |
873: | `loadAllMaterials()`             | -          | Promise | Fetch posted materials across all active courses within the date cutoff and render.                |
874: | `fetchCourseMaterials(courseId)` | string     | Promise | Fetch posted materials for a specific course ID.                                                   |
875: | `saveJsonCache(dataType, data)`  | string, arr| void    | Serialize structured Classroom state into local/session JSON cache.                                |
876: | `getJsonCache()`                 | -          | obj|null | Retrieve and parse the cached JSON snapshot from storage.                                          |
877: | `clearJsonCache()`               | -          | void    | Clear local and session JSON template cache entries.                                               |
878: | `updateBottomCachedFooter(show, timeLabel)` | bool, str | void | Toggle and render the persistent bottom cached content banner and Reconnect button.               |
879: | `openUnifiedView()`              | -          | void    | Reset `currentCourseId` to `null` and return to the default unified view feed.                     |
880: | `toggleArchivedCourses()`        | -          | void    | Toggle visibility of archived classroom cards in the "My Classes" view.                            |
881: | `toggleItemExpand(event, btn)`   | Event, Elem| void    | Toggle inline expansion of truncated description/text details for a Classroom list item.           |
882: | `copyItemText(event, btn)`       | Event, Elem| Promise | Copy item title/caption text to system clipboard with temporary checkmark visual feedback.           |
883: | `handleItemClick(event, link)`   | Event, Link| void    | Delegate row clicks to open the item URL while ignoring clicks on nested buttons and links.        |
884: | `renderItemAttachments(materials)`| Array     | string  | Render Google Drive files, YouTube videos, web links, and forms as clickable attachment pills.     |
885: | `getAssignmentStatusInfo(item)`  | object     | object  | Compute detailed status badge info (`label`, `className`, `icon`) for Missing, Assigned, Turned in, and Returned/Graded states. |
886: | `sortAssignments(assignments)`   | Array      | Array   | Sort assignments into vertical groups (Upcoming deadlines first, Assigned with no due date, Past deadline, Completed below) in ascending due dates. |
887: | `syncTurnedInAssignmentsToUserCompletions(assignments)` | Array | Promise | Automatically mark matching tasks in Pending Tasks as checked/completed for turned-in or graded assignments. |
888: | `updateLogoutButtonVisibility()` | -          | void    | Dynamically show or hide header Sign Out buttons based on connection state.                        |
889: | `syncAssignmentsToTasks()`       | -          | void    | (Admin/CR only) Sync assignments to main task list with two-way additions and deadline updates.    |
890: | `logout()`                       | -          | void    | Explicitly revoke OAuth token, clear local storage connection flags, clear cache, and reset state. |
891: | `cleanupSession()`               | -          | void    | Reset module memory state and render initial login screen.                                         |
892: | `close()`                        | Close the calendar modal.                                                                                                                                    |
893: | `renderCalendar()`               | Main render function; delegates to `generateCalendarGrid` (desktop), `renderMonthlyViewMobile` or `renderWeeklyView` (mobile, based on `currentMobileView`). |
894: | `renderMonthlyViewMobile()`      | Renders the compact monthly grid for mobile with toggle, day headers, date cells, dot indicators, and task list panel.                                       |
895: | `renderWeeklyView()`             | Renders the weekly scrolling view for mobile with toggle buttons.                                                                                            |

(2) File downloads in notes now work directly without opening new tab (added `download` attribute to file.io links). (3) Automatic session timeout - users are logged out after 1 hour of inactivity for security, with activity-based timer reset. (4) Role badges - CR and Faculty contributors now have colored badges in task cards and contribution list. (5) Mobile notifications fixed - now use Service Worker API for iOS Safari and Chrome on Android compatibility, with vibration and badge support. Updated `index.html`, `js/utils.js`, `js/auth.js`, `js/app.js`, `js/ui.js`, `js/notification-manager.js`, `sw.js`, `css/components.css`. |
1739: | 2.27.0   | Feb 2026    | File Upload Service Migration: Migrated from tmpfiles.org to file.io API for note file uploads. Updated `js/notes.js` (renamed `uploadToTmpFiles` to `uploadToFileIO`), `js/utils.js` (updated download link detection), `index.html` (updated upload instructions), `doc/DOCUMENTATION.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
1740: | 2.28.0   | Feb 2026    | Activity Timeline & User Counter: Added interactive activity heatmap and bar chart to visualize user productivity (logins, tasks, events). 1741: | 2.29.0   | Feb 2026    | Session Management & UI Improvements: Added "Stay logged in" checkbox to persist session on trusted devices. 1742: | 2.30.0   | Feb 2026    | Password Reset System Overhaul: (1) **Admin Fix**: Refactored Admin Password Reset to use Client SDK (`Auth.sendPasswordResetEmail`) instead of backend Cloud Function, effectively bootstrapping a workaround for CORS issues on the `sendPasswordReset` endpoint. (2) **Conflict Resolution**: Renamed `handlePasswordReset` to `handleAdminPasswordReset` in `app.js` to fix naming collision that broke the "Forgot Password" modal. (3) **UI Enhancements**: Added "Forgot Password" link on login failure and a new "Reset Password" button in Profile Settings. (4) **UX**: Improved visual prominence of reset links.                                                                                                                                                                   |
1743: | 2.31.0   | Feb 2026    | CR Notice Creation Fix: Fixed CRs and Admins being unable to post class notices. **(Bug)** `cr-notice.js` read user profile from non-existent `localStorage` keys (`userDepartment`, `userSemester`, `userSection`) instead of `Utils.storage.get('userProfile')`. **(Fix)** `subscribeToNotices()` and `submitNotice()` now read profile via `Utils.storage.get('userProfile')`. Removed Department/Semester/Section dropdowns from Add Notice form (auto-filled from profile). Updated Firestore rules to allow Admins to create notices. Files: `js/cr-notice.js`, `index.html`, `firestore.rules`.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
1744: | 2.33.0   | Feb 2026    | UI and Responsive Fixes: Fixed Activity Timeline bar chart clickability issue on zoomed displays (switched from Chart.js built-in onClick to robust index-based interaction mode). Fixed "All Resources" mobile center alignment.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
1745: | 2.34.0   | Feb 2026    | Classroom Sync to Tasks Feature: Added "Sync to Tasks" button functionality for Admins and CRs in the Google Classroom To-Do interface. 1746: | 2.35.0   | Feb 2026    | Dark Theme Overhaul: Implemented a new dark theme based on the Realtime Colors palette (--text: #e7f0dc; --background: #000000; --primary: #badd93; --secondary: #578323; --accent: #89d134;). Updated `css/colors.css` with new primary, secondary, accent, and text colors. Updated `css/styles.css`, `css/main.css`, `css/responsive.css`, and `css/calendar.css` to integrate the new color scheme across the entire application. 1747: | 2.36.0   | Feb 2026    | Google Classroom API now loads content from All Courses right after Sign In, instead of loading the enrolled courses.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
1748: | 2.37.0   | Feb 2026    | **Theming & UX Overhaul**: 1. **Gray Mode Theme**: Added a new monochromatic theme; set as the default for system dark mode preferences. 2. **Overdue Task Styling**: Redesigned overdue backgrounds for dark/gray modes to ensure clarity without excessive brightness (`rgba(220, 53, 69, 0.15)`). 3. **Classroom UI Theming**: Full theme integration for Google Classroom components in Gray Mode. 4. **FOUC Prevention**: Enhanced inline script to handle Gray Mode initialization.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
1749: | 2.38.0   | Feb 2026    | **Bug Fixes & UI Enhancements**: (1) Notice Refresh: Added "Refresh Notices" button to UI that passes `?refresh=true` to backend to bypass server-side caching. 1750: | 2.39.0   | Feb 2026    | **Note Section Overhaul**: (1) **PDF Export**: Integrated `html2pdf.js` for one-click note-to-PDF generation. 2445:   - Implemented dynamic rendered height measurement with `ResizeObserver` (and window `resize` fallback) setting the root CSS custom property `--offline-banner-height`.
2446:   - Configured `body.offline-active` styling across `.navbar`, `.main-content`, `.nav-center` (mobile drawer), `.auth-container`, `.set-details-container`, `.dashboard-sidebar`, mobile drawer sidebars (`#events-sidebar`, `#notice-sidebar`, `#classroom-sidebar`), and dimming backdrops (`#events-overlay`, `#notice-overlay`, `#classroom-overlay`) with `!important` offsets and `max-height` constraints so that no content or header is clipped or hidden when offline.

2448: 
2449: ### v2.46.0
2450: 
2451: - **New Feature**: **Google Classroom Real-Time Assignment Status Indicators** — Added comprehensive submission state tracking for all assignments listed under the **To-Do** section (both Unified "All Courses" and Course Detail views).
2452:   - **Student Submissions API Integration**: Extended `fetchAssignmentsData()` to concurrently query the Google Classroom Student Submissions endpoint (`courses/{courseId}/courseWork/-/studentSubmissions?userId=me`) alongside coursework.
2453:   - **Multi-State Classification**: Automatically parses submission state (`RETURNED`, `TURNED_IN`, `NEW`, `CREATED`), late submission flags (`isLate: true`), and grades (`assignedGrade` / `maxPoints`) while checking due dates against real time:
2454:     - **`Missing`** (Red label `.status-missing`, `<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-exclamation"></i>`): Assignment is past its due date and has not been submitted.
2455:     - **`Assigned`** (Blue label `.status-assigned`, `<i class="fa-solid fa-clock"></i>`): Active upcoming assignment awaiting student submission.
2456:     - **`Turned in`** / **`Turned in (Late)`** (Green label `.status-turned-in`, `<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check"></i>`): Successfully submitted by the student.
2457:     - **`Returned`** / **`Graded: X/Y`** (Purple label `.status-returned`, `<i class="fa-solid fa-award"></i>`): Evaluated and returned by the teacher, displaying points earned when available.
2458:   - **Dynamic Status Helper (`getAssignmentStatusInfo`)**: Generates consistent status labels, badge classes, and icons for both fresh API results and persistent offline JSON cache items.
2459:   - **Cache & Merge Sensitivity**: Enhanced `mergeAndSkipUnchanged()` to check `existing.status === item.status`, ensuring changes in assignment submission status immediately invalidate stale cache rows and refresh the UI.
2460:   - **Auto-Sync to Pending Tasks (`syncTurnedInAssignmentsToUserCompletions`)**: Whenever the user signs into Classroom or loads classroom contents, assignments detected as `Turned in`, `Turned in (Late)`, `Returned`, or `Graded` automatically match and mark corresponding tasks in the user's "Pending Tasks" list as completed/checked in Firestore and UI.
2461:   - **Classroom Sync Two-Way Add & Update (`syncAssignmentsToTasks`)**: Enhanced the Admin/CR Sync feature so that clicking **Sync** not only adds new assignments to the main task list, but also automatically updates existing synced tasks if a teacher modifies deadlines, titles, or descriptions in Google Classroom.
2462:   - **Classroom Desktop Minimum Width & Overflow Protection**: Enforced an 800px minimum width (`min-width: 800px;`) on `.classroom-modal-content` with horizontal scroll support on `#classroom-modal`, ensuring cards, action buttons, and course details are never squeezed on compact desktop or laptop displays.
2463:   - **Pill UI & Theming**: Fixed at the bottom-right corner of each assignment card (`bottom: 10px; right: 16px;`) with dedicated light, dark, and monochromatic gray theme styling.
2464: 
2465: ### v2.45.0

2468: - **Enhancement**: **Changelog Modal Theme Colors & Vivid Badges** — Enhanced font brightness and contrast in dark and gray modes for changelog descriptions, dates, and subtitle texts. Added bright, high-contrast category badges for New Feature, Fix & Enhancement, Security, and Major updates.
2469: - **Enhancement**: **Calendar Desktop Minimum Width & Cell Sizing** — Enforced an 820px minimum width on the desktop calendar modal content and 780px on the calendar grid with horizontal overflow support, preventing task items and course names from being squeezed on compact displays.
2470: - **Fix & Enhancement**: **Protruding Floating Close Button & Unclipped Corner** — Redesigned the calendar modal close button to float over the top-right corner with 360-degree rotation and scale micro-interactions. Eliminated container corner clipping by enforcing visible overflow and elevated z-index.
2471: - **Enhancement**: **Theme-Adaptive Month & Year Dropdown Chevrons** — Replaced harsh red dropdown arrows with sleek, modern stroke chevrons tuned to match active Light, Dark (lime green), and Gray (lavender/silver) themes.
2472: - **Enhancement**: **Hidden Scrollbars Across Calendar Window** — Applied cross-browser scrollbar hiding across the calendar grid, weekly views, mobile date lists, and task detail modals while retaining full smooth scrolling via touch, mouse wheel, and trackpad.

The following code has been modified to include a line number before every line, in the format: <line_number>: <original_line>. Please note that any changes targeting the original code should remove the line number, colon, and leading space.
848: - **Archived Classrooms Management**: Fetches both active and archived courses (`courseStates=ACTIVE&courseStates=ARCHIVED`). Archived classrooms are hidden by default in the "My Classes" view, with a toggle button to expand/hide them. Unified feeds (**To-Do**, **Notices**, **Materials**) strictly filter out content from archived courses.
849: - **Client JSON Template Caching & Persistent Reconnect Footer**: Serializes user courses, assignments, notices, and materials into a structured JSON template (`classroom_cached_json`) saved to `localStorage` (mirrored in `sessionStorage`) for persistent offline access across browser tab closures and token expirations. When cached content is displayed due to an expired session, a sticky bottom footer bar (`#classroom-footer-mobile` and `#classroom-footer-desktop`) is rendered with a narrow banner (`"Cached Content, login again to see new data"`) and a persistent `"Reconnect Classroom"` button (`Classroom.login()`).
850: - **Real-Time Assignment Status Indicators**: Concurrently queries Google Classroom Student Submissions (`courses/{courseId}/courseWork/-/studentSubmissions?userId=me`) to render color-coded bottom-right badges on all To-Do items: **Assigned** (Blue), **Missing** (Red), **Turned in** / **Turned in (Late)** (Green), and **Returned** / **Graded: X/Y** (Purple).
851: - **Two-Group Vertical To-Do Arrangement**: Automatically groups assignments into two vertical sections: active/pending assignments at the top (so they are always immediately visible), followed by completed/turned-in assignments below, each sorted in ascending order of due dates.
852: - **Automatic Task Completion Checking**: Upon sign-in or loading classroom contents, assignments in `Turned in`, `Returned`, or `Graded` state automatically match and check/complete corresponding tasks in the user's personal "Pending Tasks" list.
853: - **Two-Way Task Sync (Add & Update)**: Clicking **Sync** for Admins/CRs creates new tasks from assignments and automatically updates existing tasks if a teacher modifies deadlines, titles, or instructions in Classroom.
854: - **Materials Change Detection**: Live updates to course materials (new Drive files, YouTube links, Forms, or descriptions) are dynamically recognized via `updateTime` comparison and rendered as interactive attachment pills.
855: 
856: **Properties:**

@@ -847,10 +847,12 @@
 - **Unified Sign Out Button**: A single pill-styled Sign Out button (`.classroom-header-logout-btn`, `border-radius: 20px`) is embedded in top window title headers (`#classroom-sidebar` mobile and `#classroom-modal` desktop). The button is dynamically displayed only when logged in / connected. Explicit logout revokes the OAuth token, clears local storage connection flags, clears cache, and resets the view to the login prompt.
 - **Archived Classrooms Management**: Fetches both active and archived courses (`courseStates=ACTIVE&courseStates=ARCHIVED`). Archived classrooms are hidden by default in the "My Classes" view, with a toggle button to expand/hide them. Unified feeds (**To-Do**, **Notices**, **Materials**) strictly filter out content from archived courses.
 - **Client JSON Template Caching & Persistent Reconnect Footer**: Serializes user courses, assignments, notices, and materials into a structured JSON template (`classroom_cached_json`) saved to `localStorage` (mirrored in `sessionStorage`) for persistent offline access across browser tab closures and token expirations. When cached content is displayed due to an expired session, a sticky bottom footer bar (`#classroom-footer-mobile` and `#classroom-footer-desktop`) is rendered with a narrow banner (`"Cached Content, login again to see new data"`) and a persistent `"Reconnect Classroom"` button (`Classroom.login()`).
-- **Real-Time Assignment Status Indicators**: Concurrently queries Google Classroom Student Submissions (`courses/{courseId}/courseWork/-/studentSubmissions?userId=me`) to render color-coded bottom-right badges on all To-Do items: **Assigned** (Blue), **Missing** (Red), **Turned in** / **Turned in (Late)** (Green), and **Returned** / **Graded: X/Y** (Purple).
-- **Two-Group Vertical To-Do Arrangement**: Automatically groups assignments into two vertical sections: active/pending assignments at the top (so they are always immediately visible), followed by completed/turned-in assignments below, each sorted in ascending order of due dates.
-- **Automatic Task Completion Checking**: Upon sign-in or loading classroom contents, assignments in `Turned in`, `Returned`, or `Graded` state automatically match and check/complete corresponding tasks in the user's personal "Pending Tasks" list.
-- **Two-Way Task Sync (Add & Update)**: Clicking **Sync** for Admins/CRs creates new tasks from assignments and automatically updates existing tasks if a teacher modifies deadlines, titles, or instructions in Classroom.
+- **Vertical To-Do Multi-Group Hierarchy & Deadline Priority**: Automatically organizes assignments into hierarchical vertical sections:
+  1. **Active Upcoming Deadlines**: Tasks with future due dates prioritized at the very top, sorted chronologically (earliest deadline first) so upcoming work is immediately actionable.
+  2. **Assigned (No Due Date)**: Tasks without deadlines listed directly below upcoming deadlines.
+  3. **Past Deadline Tasks**: Unsubmitted/overdue tasks grouped under a compact `"Past deadline"` divider banner with task count badge (`<i class="fa-solid fa-clock-rotate-left"></i>`). The banner is conditionally shown when active upcoming/assigned tasks exist above it.
+  4. **Completed Tasks**: Submitted, graded, or returned tasks grouped under a `"Completed"` divider banner (`<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check"></i>`), sub-divided so completed tasks whose deadline is still open appear first, followed by a `"Past deadline"` sub-banner and completed tasks submitted late/past deadline.
+- **Desktop Window Border Padding Elimination**: Excludes `#classroom-modal .modal-content` and `.classroom-modal-content` from default modal padding on 769px–1366px displays and height-constrained screens, rendering flush without border clipping or nested whitespace.
 - **Materials Change Detection**: Live updates to course materials (new Drive files, YouTube links, Forms, or descriptions) are dynamically recognized via `updateTime` comparison and rendered as interactive attachment pills.

1750: | 2.37.0   | Feb 2026    | **Theming & UX Overhaul**: 1. **Gray Mode Theme**: Added a new monochromatic theme; set as the default for system dark mode preferences. 2. **Overdue Task Styling**: Redesigned overdue backgrounds for dark/gray modes to ensure clarity without excessive brightness (`rgba(220, 53, 69, 0.15)`). 3. **Classroom UI Theming**: Full theme integration for Google Classroom components in Gray Mode. 4. **FOUC Prevention**: Enhanced inline script to handle Gray Mode initialization.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
1751: | 2.38.0   | Feb 2026    | **Bug Fixes & UI Enhancements**: (1) Notice Refresh: Added "Refresh Notices" button to UI that passes `?refresh=true` to backend to bypass server-side caching. (2) Mobile Zoom & Canvas Coordinate Fix: Applied dynamic JS-based 95% zoom (`document.body.style.zoom`) for screens <= 768px, with specific resets in `timeline-ui.js` to preserve `Chart.js` canvas coordinate integrity when modal is active. (3) Empty states: Added `"No upcoming events"` fallback for mobile Events sidebar. (4) Notes Modals: Fixed Note Preview infinitely growing by enforcing `max-height: 48vh;` internal scrolling. (5) Section Guides: Added hoverable/clickable tooltips (`<i class="fas fa-info-circle">`) to main feature headers explaining their purpose for new users.                       |
1752: | 2.39.0   | Feb 2026    | **Note Section Overhaul**: (1) **PDF Export**: Integrated `html2pdf.js` for one-click note-to-PDF generation. (2) **Automatic ZIP Fallback**: Integrated `JSZip` to client-side compress unsupported file types (e.g., `.sh`, `.exe`) into `.zip` blobs before upload. (3) **Layout Optimization**: Removed fixed `max-height` constraints in `css/note.css` to enable full flexbox expansion, eliminating whitespace gaps in the note modal editor. (4) **CORS Fix**: Re-prioritized `file.io` as the lead upload provider to bypass `catbox.moe` CORS restrictions on certain domains.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

 | 2.30.0   | Feb 2026    | Password Reset System Overhaul: (1) **Admin Fix**: Refactored Admin Password Reset to use Client SDK (`Auth.sendPasswordResetEmail`) instead of backend Cloud Function, effectively bootstrapping a workaround for CORS issues on the `sendPasswordReset` endpoint. (2) **Conflict Resolution**: Renamed `handlePasswordReset` to `handleAdminPasswordReset` in `app.js` to fix naming collision that broke the "Forgot Password" modal. (3) **UI Enhancements**: Added "Forgot Password" link on login failure and a new "Reset Password" button in Profile Settings. (4) **UX**: Improved visual prominence of reset links.                                                                                                                                                                   |
 | 2.31.0   | Feb 2026    | CR Notice Creation Fix: Fixed CRs and Admins being unable to post class notices. **(Bug)** `cr-notice.js` read user profile from non-existent `localStorage` keys (`userDepartment`, `userSemester`, `userSection`) instead of `Utils.storage.get('userProfile')`. **(Fix)** `subscribeToNotices()` and `submitNotice()` now read profile via `Utils.storage.get('userProfile')`. Removed Department/Semester/Section dropdowns from Add Notice form (auto-filled from profile). Updated Firestore rules to allow Admins to create notices. Files: `js/cr-notice.js`, `index.html`, `firestore.rules`.                                                                                                                                                                                          |

+| 2.36.0   | Feb 2026    | Google Classroom API now loads content from All Courses right after Sign In, instead of loading the enrolled courses.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
 | 2.37.0   | Feb 2026    | **Theming & UX Overhaul**: 1. **Gray Mode Theme**: Added a new monochromatic theme; set as the default for system dark mode preferences. 2. **Overdue Task Styling**: Redesigned overdue backgrounds for dark/gray modes to ensure clarity without excessive brightness (`rgba(220, 53, 69, 0.15)`). 3. **Classroom UI Theming**: Full theme integration for Google Classroom components in Gray Mode. 4. **FOUC Prevention**: Enhanced inline script to handle Gray Mode initialization.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
 | 2.38.0   | Feb 2026    | **Bug Fixes & UI Enhancements**: (1) Notice Refresh: Added "Refresh Notices" button to UI that passes `?refresh=true` to backend to bypass server-side caching. (2) Mobile Zoom & Canvas Coordinate Fix: Applied dynamic JS-based 95% zoom (`document.body.style.zoom`) for screens <= 768px, with specific resets in `timeline-ui.js` to preserve `Chart.js` canvas coordinate integrity when modal is active. (3) Empty states: Added `"No upcoming events"` fallback for mobile Events sidebar. (4) Notes Modals: Fixed Note Preview infinitely growing by enforcing `max-height: 48vh;` internal scrolling. (5) Section Guides: Added hoverable/clickable tooltips (`<i class="fas fa-info-circle">`) to main feature headers explaining their purpose for new users.                       |
 | 2.39.0   | Feb 2026    | **Note Section Overhaul**: (1) **PDF Export**: Integrated `html2pdf.js` for one-click note-to-PDF generation. (2) **Automatic ZIP Fallback**: Integrated `JSZip` to client-side compress unsupported file types (e.g., `.sh`, `.exe`) into `.zip` blobs before upload. (3) **Layout Optimization**: Removed fixed `max-height` constraints in `css/note.css` to enable full flexbox expansion, eliminating whitespace gaps in the note modal editor. (4) **CORS Fix**: Re-prioritized `file.io` as the lead upload provider to bypass `catbox.moe` CORS restrictions on certain domains.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

1750: | 2.37.0   | Feb 2026    | **Theming & UX Overhaul**: 1. **Gray Mode Theme**: Added a new monochromatic theme; set as the default for system dark mode preferences. 2. **Overdue Task Styling**: Redesigned overdue backgrounds for dark/gray modes to ensure clarity without excessive brightness (`rgba(220, 53, 69, 0.15)`). 3. **Classroom UI Theming**: Full theme integration for Google Classroom components in Gray Mode. 4. **FOUC Prevention**: Enhanced inline script to handle Gray Mode initialization.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
1751: | 2.38.0   | Feb 2026    | **Bug Fixes & UI Enhancements**: (1) Notice Refresh: Added "Refresh Notices" button to UI that passes `?refresh=true` to backend to bypass server-side caching. (2) Mobile Zoom & Canvas Coordinate Fix: Applied dynamic JS-based 95% zoom (`document.body.style.zoom`) for screens <= 768px, with specific resets in `timeline-ui.js` to preserve `Chart.js` canvas coordinate integrity when modal is active. (3) Empty states: Added `"No upcoming events"` fallback for mobile Events sidebar. (4) Notes Modals: Fixed Note Preview infinitely growing by enforcing `max-height: 48vh;` internal scrolling. (5) Section Guides: Added hoverable/clickable tooltips (`<i class="fas fa-info-circle">`) to main feature headers explaining their purpose for new users.                       |
1752: | 2.39.0   | Feb 2026    | **Note Section Overhaul**: (1) **PDF Export**: Integrated `html2pdf.js` for one-click note-to-PDF generation. (2) **Automatic ZIP Fallback**: Integrated `JSZip` to client-side compress unsupported file types (e.g., `.sh`, `.exe`) into `.zip` blobs before upload. (3) **Layout Optimization**: Removed fixed `max-height` constraints in `css/note.css` to enable full flexbox expansion, eliminating whitespace gaps in the note modal editor. (4) **CORS Fix**: Re-prioritized `file.io` as the lead upload provider to bypass `catbox.moe` CORS restrictions on certain domains.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
1753: | 2.40.0   | Mar 2026    | PWA Offline Improvements: Resolved view loading issues in offline mode; fixed Google Classroom caching persistence across views; implemented Vercel Blob storage for backend file serving.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
1754: | 2.41.0   | Mar 11 2026 | Enhanced Markdown Support: Rewrote rendering pipeline to support fenced code blocks (```), <pre> tags, and safe HTML entities (math/Greek symbols) globally across tasks, events, notices, and notes. Improved CSS for code block visibility.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
1755: | 2.44.0   | Aug 2026    | Google Classroom Materials Tab & Client JSON Caching: Added Materials view tab, full client JSON template caching (`classroom_cached_json`), unified pre-fetching with incremental skip tracking, persistent bottom reconnect banner, and row copy actions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
1756: | 2.45.0   | Aug 2026    | Dynamic Department & Metadata Options: Server-priority fetching for Firestore metadata, multi-structure document support, dynamic event department selectors, and comprehensive faculty filters.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
1757: | 2.46.0   | Aug 2026    | Google Classroom Assignment Status Badges: Integrated real-time student submission tracking (`studentSubmissions?userId=me`) to render distinct bottom-right status badges on all To-Do assignments for Missing (Red), Assigned (Blue), Turned in (Green), and Returned / Graded: X/Y (Purple) across all color themes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
1758: | 2.47.0   | Aug 2026    | UITS Event Raiders Live Feed Integration: Syndicated live hackathons, contests, olympiads, and symposiums from `ou1ts/events` RSS/JSON feeds (`js/raids-feed.js`). Organized desktop sidebar with dedicated vertical separation below upcoming events, and integrated a vertically centered solid maroon right-arrowhead view switcher on the mobile events drawer.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
